029

Avid PRO CNC + EX 24.1 servo

Contact assignments

Main wiring diagram · SmoothieBox

Why this controller: Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate.

Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior command contacts feed the depicted buffered interface into retained ClearPath servos through the Avid interconnect peripherals. Qualify 24 V sensor, tool-setting, spindle and auxiliary interfaces separately.

Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate. Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior case contacts with retained external power stages. The main diagram and connection-ID schedule define the current proposed routes. Dotted GUESS routes need the named commissioning checks; OPEN routes remain disconnected until the missing terminal, rating or interface is established. Previous Core alternatives remain only in the superseded historical architecture record.

Current installation sequence
  1. Match the actual machine and controller revisions, trace the unpowered harness and verify all signal/reference, power and independent safety boundaries before energising. Follow the current connection IDs rather than the superseded architecture prose.
  2. Smoothieboard V2 SmoothieBox supply inlet: P1 J42: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. SmoothieBox P1 J42.1 and J42.2 exported nets are /5VPORTIN and GND. J42.1 is an input contact, not a supply output; actual acceptable supply range is a revision-specific commissioning check.
  3. ClearPath command buffer #1: SmoothieBox STEP/DIR → AHCT125 → Avid J8/J9: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. TI SN74AHCT125 pin map shown for 14-pin D/N/NS/PW family: pin14 +5V, pin7 GND; gates (1A pin2→1Y pin3, /OE pin1; 2A pin5→2Y pin6, /OE pin4; 3A pin9→3Y pin8, /OE pin10; 4A pin12→4Y pin11, /OE pin13). /OE low passes data; set enables only with reviewed power-up/reset behavior. Chip must be decoupled at its supply. Explicit supply proposal: each AHCT125 pin14 receives a dedicated regulated +5V rail, pin7 returns to common star, with 100nF ceramic placed directly from pin14 to pin7. All /OE pins are active-low and shown tied to GND (always enabled) only as a test-gated proposal; confirm boot waveform and keep servo power disabled until output is stable. Grounds are represented once per physical connector contact.
  4. ClearPath command buffer #2: SmoothieBox STEP/DIR → AHCT125 → Avid J8/J9: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. TI SN74AHCT125 pin map shown for 14-pin D/N/NS/PW family: pin14 +5V, pin7 GND; gates (1A pin2→1Y pin3, /OE pin1; 2A pin5→2Y pin6, /OE pin4; 3A pin9→3Y pin8, /OE pin10; 4A pin12→4Y pin11, /OE pin13). /OE low passes data; set enables only with reviewed power-up/reset behavior. Chip must be decoupled at its supply. Explicit supply proposal: each AHCT125 pin14 receives a dedicated regulated +5V rail, pin7 returns to common star, with 100nF ceramic placed directly from pin14 to pin7. All /OE pins are active-low and shown tied to GND (always enabled) only as a test-gated proposal; confirm boot waveform and keep servo power disabled until output is stable. Grounds are represented once per physical connector contact.
  5. Three AHCT125 devices: explicit +5V, return and bypass paths: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. SN74AHCT125 datasheet identifies pin14=VCC, pin7=GND, operating supply 4.5–5.5V. This panel gives each of the three devices its own explicit supply pins and local 100nF capacitor. A common return star is a wiring proposal, not source-established isolation behavior; only join SmoothieBox returns and Avid J7.1 COM after verifying exact receiver grounding. The individual driver panels additionally show each signal input, active-low OE, output and pin map.
  6. Drive enable candidate: third AHCT125 channel: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. AHCT125 #3: pin14=regulated +5V, pin7=GND, 100nF local bypass. /OE pin1 is active-low; tied to common ground in this test-gated wiring so channel passes data. Verify /MOT_EN polarity and downstream ENA behavior; this MCU signal is not an E-stop. Keep motor power disabled until no-glitch reset tests pass.
  7. Retain Avid J1 to X ClearPath motor cable: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Avid 24.1 connector schedule labels pins 1 HLFB+, 2-4 +5V, 5 HLFB-, 6 STEP, 7 DIR, 8 ENABLE. This is the Avid-to-servo cable, not SmoothieBox header wiring; preserve as documented only after revision match.
  8. Retain Avid J2 to Y1 ClearPath motor cable: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Avid 24.1 connector schedule labels pins 1 HLFB+, 2-4 +5V, 5 HLFB-, 6 STEP, 7 DIR, 8 ENABLE. This is the Avid-to-servo cable, not SmoothieBox header wiring; preserve as documented only after revision match.
  9. Retain Avid J3 to A ClearPath motor cable: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Avid 24.1 connector schedule labels pins 1 HLFB+, 2-4 +5V, 5 HLFB-, 6 STEP, 7 DIR, 8 ENABLE. This is the Avid-to-servo cable, not SmoothieBox header wiring; preserve as documented only after revision match.
  10. Retain Avid J4 to Y2 ClearPath motor cable: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Avid 24.1 connector schedule labels pins 1 HLFB+, 2-4 +5V, 5 HLFB-, 6 STEP, 7 DIR, 8 ENABLE. This is the Avid-to-servo cable, not SmoothieBox header wiring; preserve as documented only after revision match.
  11. Retain Avid J5 to Z ClearPath motor cable: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Avid 24.1 connector schedule labels pins 1 HLFB+, 2-4 +5V, 5 HLFB-, 6 STEP, 7 DIR, 8 ENABLE. This is the Avid-to-servo cable, not SmoothieBox header wiring; preserve as documented only after revision match.
  12. X− home/limit · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed XMIN assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  13. X+ limit · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed XMAX assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  14. Y− home/limit · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed YMIN assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  15. Y+ limit · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed YMAX assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  16. Z+ home/limit · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed ZMAX assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  17. Slaved Y2 home · candidate free input, not Z-min · existing M12 sensor through rated interface: Avid source: M12 pin1 24V, pin3 GND, pin4 signal; verify female/male mirrored view and actual port label. Sensor output mode and signal range must be identified. Use a 24V-rated isolated input module; do not connect sensor directly to controller GPIO. Proposed ZMIN assignment needs firmware home/limit/slaved-axis function review, not a claim about actual machine direction.
  18. Tool Height Setter / optional touch plate · Aux2/Aux1: Avid EX manual locates Tool Height Setter on Aux2 and optional corner/touch plate on Aux1. Interface is not established as a generic passive contact. Trace exact sensor supply, output and reference and choose isolated conditioning before wiring. Do not connect two sensors blindly to one probe input.
  19. Spindle speed, run and ready/fault · all separate interfaces: Official 14-pin spindle-only cable roles: 7 FWD,8 DCM,9 analog command,10 analog common,1 ready common,2 ready input. Identify installed VFD/model/connector and cable revision. Use a PWM-to-0–10V interface with matched isolated analog return; isolated dry-contact stage for run; rated isolated input for ready. These are required interface functions, not a claim that factory Acorn outputs accept arbitrary external drive.
  20. Independent E-stop and optional auxiliary power relays: Avid interconnect table gives J7.14 E-stop, J7.8/J7.10 relay returns and J7.12 +24V relay feed. Preserve an independent stop chain and identify actual relay coil/contact circuits. SSR2 is only a proposed process-control signal to a rated isolated driver, never direct AC/router power or an E-stop safety output.
  21. Trace the separate four-pin motor DC harness for all five ClearPath servos. Teknic reference: POWER 1/3 V+, 2/4 GND in wire-entry view; verify exact motor/revision and as-built distribution before installation. Keep motor current out of controller signal grounds; the eight-pin command connector does not supply motor power.
  22. Resource conflict: GF.3 is reserved by both Aux1 (C17-007) and spindle-ready (C18-011). These are mutually exclusive allocations of one input, not signals to join. Select one supported assignment or source a distinct free input before wiring; both proposed paths remain OPEN.

Teknic MC/SD manual Rev 3.30 printed page 38 defines the separate four-position power connector in WIRE ENTRY VIEW: 1 and 3 V+, 2 and 4 GND. ClearPath command buffer #1: SmoothieBox STEP/DIR → AHCT125 → Avid J8/J9SmoothieBox · exterior terminals · DRVX.5 · ENABLE · NC: receiver enable handled separately; do not attach blindlyNo conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. TI SN74AHCT125 pin map shown for 14-pin D/N/NS/PW family: pin14 +5V, pin7 GND; gates (1A pin2→1Y pin3, /OE pin1; 2A pin5→2Y pin6, /OE pin4; 3A pin9→3Y pin8, /OE pin10; 4A pin12→4Y pin11, /OE pin13). /OE low passes data; set enables only with reviewed power-up/reset behavior. Chip must be decoupled at its supply. Explicit supply proposal: each AHCT125 pin14 receives a dedicated regulated +5V rail, pin7 returns to common star, with 100nF ceramic placed directly from pin14 to pin7. All /OE pins are active-low and shown tied to GND (always enabled) only as a test-gated proposal; confirm boot waveform and keep servo power disabled until output is stable. Grounds are represented once per physical connector contact.

Spindle speed, run and ready/fault · all separate interfacesAvid 14-pin spindle peripheral · trace to fitted VFD · Retain VFD/spindle power, PE and rated cablesNo conductor drawn. Official 14-pin spindle-only cable roles: 7 FWD,8 DCM,9 analog command,10 analog common,1 ready common,2 ready input. Identify installed VFD/model/connector and cable revision. Use a PWM-to-0–10V interface with matched isolated analog return; isolated dry-contact stage for run; rated isolated input for ready. These are required interface functions, not a claim that factory Acorn outputs accept arbitrary external drive. Resource conflict: GF.3 is reserved by both Aux1 (C17-007) and spindle-ready (C18-011). These are mutually exclusive allocations of one input, not signals to join. Select one supported assignment or source a distinct free input before wiring; both proposed paths remain OPEN.

Avid interconnect table gives J7.14 E-stop, J7.8/J7.10 relay returns and J7.12 +24V relay feed. Preserve an independent stop chain and identify actual relay coil/contact circuits. SSR2 is only a proposed process-control signal to a rated isolated driver, never direct AC/router power or an E-stop safety output.

SmoothieBox Chapter 18 field-contact drawing; Avid 24.1 interconnect schedule

The earlier circuit details remain immediately below.

Earlier alternative retrofit architecture and circuit details

Owner retrofit guide · Avid EX 24.1 ClearPath retrofit

The drawing provides individual motor, command, power and peripheral conductors. Select one controller architecture from the target assessment; alternatives must not be wired at the same time. Retain only matching, measured machine harnesses and commission each circuit against its listed gate.

Which Smoothie target fits this machine?
  • SmoothieBox: Carrier/enclosure only. Place a Core controller and isolated/translated command interface inside; box does not provide servo control by itself.
  • V2 Core: Four channels of buffered 5V STEP/DIR can be proposed to the documented CRP5310-01E J8/J9 Acorn inputs. Verify the exact board, reference/isolation, setup timing, and enable behavior first.
  • V2 Prime: Prime coil outputs cannot drive servo command inputs. No direct Prime STEP/DIR source is established here; use Core or an independently specified external pulse generator.
Retain or add
  • Three TI SN74AHCT125 14-pin buffers as a test-gated level-interface proposal: two devices route eight STEP/DIR lines, one channel on the third carries an enable candidate. Select 14-pin package documented by TI; provide verified 5V, decoupling, and safe /OE control.
  • If Core return and Avid J7.1 COM cannot be safely joined, use a pulse interface that provides galvanic isolation and matches Avid receiver characteristics.
  • Retain Avid CRP5310-01E and ClearPath wiring only if installed revision/cables match the 24.1 schedule. Keep hardware E-stop independent.
Conversion and commissioning
  1. Inspect CRP board silkscreen, J8/J9/J12/J13/J10 state and motor cable options; do not infer rev from machine model.
  2. Build and bench-check each AHCT125: pin14=regulated 4.5–5.5V, pin7=GND, one 100nF directly across each IC supply pair, and all four /OE pins per chip deliberately set. Verify power-up/reset behavior with servo power disabled. Confirm Core 3.3V VIH meets TI AHCT125 VIH and output loading is within limits.
  3. Verify Core and Avid signal references; use isolated pulse interface if a safe common is not established. Never assume J7.1 COM can be tied to Core return.
  4. Connect and scope one STEP/DIR channel at a time to J8/J9; verify pulse width/setup, no boot glitches and axis mapping before servo enable.
  5. Enable only under bounded safe test; validate Y2 J12 jumper path and J13 inversion, HLFB and faults, then prove hardwired E-stop. Keep VFD/plasma disabled pending exact peripherals.

Pin-by-pin schedule and commissioning notes
  • TI SN74AHCT125 14-pin pin schedule: 1=/OE1, 2=1A, 3=1Y, 4=/OE2, 5=2A, 6=2Y, 7=GND, 8=3Y, 9=3A, 10=/OE3, 11=4Y, 12=4A, 13=/OE4, 14=VCC. Each active-low OE must be deliberately controlled; low enables A-to-Y.
  • Core→Avid: J5.16 STEP_A -> buffer1 input -> J8.1 X STEP; J5.18 DIR_A -> buffer1 -> J8.2 X DIR; J5.22 STEP_B -> buffer1 -> J8.3 Y STEP; J5.24 DIR_B -> buffer1 -> J8.4 Y DIR; J5.28 STEP_C -> buffer2 -> J9.1 Z STEP; J5.30 DIR_C -> buffer2 -> J9.2 Z DIR; J5.34 STEP_D -> buffer2 -> J9.3 A STEP; J5.36 DIR_D -> buffer2 -> J9.4 A DIR. All eight routes are proposals until current input references/timing are verified.
  • Enable candidate: Core J5.13 /MOT_EN -> third AHCT125 channel -> Avid J12.1 ENA1; polarity/fanout/reset state unresolved. J12.2/3 are jumper-derived Y step routes, not enable.
  • Avid servo cable connectors J1=X,J2=Y1,J3=A,J4=Y2,J5=Z each have pins1 HLFB+,2-4 +5V,5 HLFB-,6 STEP,7 DIR,8 ENABLE per Avid 24.1 source. This is retained Avid-side wiring, not direct Core wiring.
  • Smoothieboard V2 Core P1 J42 candidate supply inlet: pin1=/5VPORTIN, pin2=GND (exported board contacts). Verify exact board-revision supply specifications before connection; pin1 is not a 5V output.

Avid publishes 14-pin peripheral functions: Spindle OK, plasma torch and divided voltage (option dependent), spindle forward, DCM and analog speed. The stepper variant publishes a separate DB9/XLR phase map that does not apply to this servo harness. The current drawing separates the 14 cable positions from CRP5310-01E servo board J1–J9/J12 source contacts; neither establishes a fitted harness.

The EX stepper variant publishes a separate DB9/XLR motor-phase map. That map does not apply to this servo motor or its encoder/feedback harness.

Interface reading

Signal domains: Motion control or motor phases, limits/probe, spindle and safety where fitted. Controller family: Avid EX 14-pin.

What is still missing

Plasma-only cable omits Spindle OK contacts 1–2; servo harness is not the stepper DB9/XLR map. Confirm EX lid revision, option cable population and safety chain.

Documented evidence · unresolved wiring

Documented: Avid EX 24.1 servo schematics, official page 2026Q2.1; CRP5210 interconnect plus CRP5310-01E servo board, a different motor-control domain from #027. The 14-pin spindle/plasma cable retains 1/2 Spindle OK, 3/4 torch, 5/6 divided voltage, 7/8 relay FWD/DCM, 9/10 analog speed/common.
